  - **Owner identity (split per SP UI engineer 2026-06-12 review)** — TWO email fields plus identifier:
    - `owner_corp_usa_email` (SP `STR` free-form text per `[D-086]`; TPM-typed corp-USA email; HILDA does NOT validate against AD; **TPM-editable inline `[Ph-2]`** per FR-3 / DEF-22 + FR-14 Option B; **Ph-1 fixed at `setup_milestone` time per FR-2 R&R lock**)
    - `owner_corp_email` (SP `STR` free-form text; typically used for non-USA owners or external collaborators; **TPM-editable inline `[Ph-2]`** — same Ph-2 deferral)
    - `owner_corp_id` (SP `STR` free-form text; corp directory identifier — TPM types whatever; HILDA does NOT auto-derive from email local-part per `[D-086]`; **TPM-editable inline `[Ph-2]`** — same Ph-2 deferral; `owner_corp_id` change alone is NOT an owner-change event per FR-88)
    - `owner_name` (SP `STR` free-form text per `[D-086]`; TPM types display name; HILDA does NOT resolve via AD lookup; **TPM-editable inline `[Ph-2]`** — same Ph-2 deferral)
    - HILDA preference rule (per FR-9 + `[D-080]`): use `owner_corp_usa_email` if set; else `owner_corp_email`. Either email change = owner-change event (fires `OwnerReassigned` rule per FR-28 Ph-2).
  - `delivery_state` (**11-value SP Choice column** = 10-value Python `DeliveryState` enum + `Not Started` SP-only pre-tracking state per FR-7 10-vs-11 distinction; HILDA-managed via state transitions; **at tracker creation** SP UI engineer's `setup_milestone` writes `Not Started` (per FR-2 R&R lock; HILDA does NOT write this value); **on Start Collection click** HILDA receives the SP-alert and writes back `delivery_state = "Open"` (HILDA state machine activates from that point). **Read-only via list-view cell display** for normal state transitions — most changes go via per-row buttons: Approve (UnderPMReview → ReadyForSubmission), Mark Closed (path iii/iv → Closed), Confirm Carrier Acceptance (SubmittedToCustomer → Closed), Resume from Delayed/Blocked (Delayed/Blocked → prior state per FR-7 I2). **TPM direct cell-edit via FR-14 is RESERVED for two residual use cases**: (1) setting `Delayed` or `Blocked` from an active state when TPM observes an external delay/blocker before owner reports (HILDA's `sp_alert_parser` auto-sets `prior_delivery_state` per FR-7 I2 / FR-12 / FR-14 discipline); (2) rolling back an erroneously-fired automated transition (e.g., HILDA wrongly advanced to `ReadyForSubmission` — TPM rolls back to `UnderPMReview`). All other state changes go via dedicated buttons per `docs/sp_ui_engineer/SP_UI_button_actions.md` Part 1)
  - (No per-item `expected_completion_date` column on `Deliverables_<customer_id>` per `[D-085]` — `Milestone.target_date` is the sole authoritative deadline; SP UI displays milestone target_date at row render time by reading the Milestones SP list row for the milestone.)
  - `review_required` (Boolean; YAML default; **TPM-editable inline** per FR-2 — gates LLM quality review per FR-53; MUST be False on Confirmation items per FR-58)
  - ~~`item_description` (FR-82 comma-separated tag list)~~ — **MOVED to optional display 2026-06-12 per SP UI engineer review**; ops-managed only (HILDA routing internal), not TPM-editable; see optional-display block (b) below
  - `email_cc_list` (per-item CC override; YAML default pre-populated from per-TG `default_cc_list`; **TPM-editable inline** per FR-2)
  - `comment` (free-form PM commentary; **TPM-editable inline**)
  - `tracking_modality` (multi-value MultiChoice; per-item override of TG default; YAML default; **TPM-editable inline** per FR-2)
  - `manual_triage_required` (Boolean; HILDA-set on FR-12 path (c.2) below-threshold owner-reply LLM classification; **TPM-clearable inline or via Clear Triage Flag button per FR-12 I4 + FR-14**)
  - ~~`per_item_rule_overrides`~~ — **MOVED to Ph-2 deferred per 2026-06-12 SP UI engineer review**; Ph-1 TPMs request rule overrides via ops who edit `customizations/rules/<customer_id>/per_item_overrides.yaml` (YAML drop-zone, picked up via SIGHUP reload); SP UI surface for per-item rule overrides revisited in Ph-2 with concrete TPM-requested override columns (likely 4-5 discrete fields per Option (β) of 2026-06-12 review: `reminder_interval_override_days`, `escalation_threshold_override_days`, `stale_alert_suppressed`, `all_rules_paused`, optional `paused_rule_ids`)
  - `milestone_gating` (Boolean; YAML-loaded; **NOT TPM-editable** — ops/PM team locks at template creation; governs FR-64 Close All Items enablement per FR-64 rewrite 2026-06-12)

  **(b) Optional display — read-only fields visible by default or available via row expansion** (NOT TPM-editable; HILDA-managed or YAML-locked):
  - HILDA-managed (written by HILDA via `[D-064]` SP REST writeback): `actual_completion_date`, `last_owner_contacted`, `last_owner_response_at` (FR-10 I4 — set by FR-12 inbound parsing on owner reply; HILDA's "no response" signal for FR-10 escalation), `reminder_count` (FR-10 I3 — incremented on every successful reminder/escalation dispatch), `prior_delivery_state` (FR-7 I2 — set when entering Delayed/Blocked; cleared on exit), `review_status` (4-value enum: `pending` / `complete` / `not_required` / `failed` per FR-53 I8 lock — `failed` added 2026-06-16 for persistent LLM review failure), `item_completion_pct` (FR-70 progress), `owner_status_note` (auto-populated from inbound owner replies)

Note `tg_owner_corp_email` does NOT exist per `[D-086]` (never supported even in prior version). **All denormalized TG fields are READ-ONLY on delivery-item rows** — TG-level values are sourced from customer YAML at DI creation per `[D-064]` writeback; TPM editing on a delivery-item row would diverge from siblings + YAML; typically rendered in the TG group header above each tg_name group, not as per-row columns. TG-value changes go through customer YAML edit + HILDA tracker rewriting delivery-item rows for the affected TG (Ph-1 deferred SP-side TG editing UI per `customizations/sharepoint_config/MODULE.md` 2026-06-12)

  **(c) Per-row buttons** (item-level actions; NOT field edits):

HILDA's `/docs/<customer_id>/<item_id>` endpoint resolves to a row via `Deliverables_<customer_id>` `GetItemById(<item_id>)` and renders the document section per FR-57 / FR-59. Visible only when `item_type ≠ Confirmation`
  - **View in PLM** — link to `actual_item_info` (PLM issue URL); null for items without PLM issue (pre-first-document state)

  - `[Ph-2]` **Upload Document** — link-out anchor to `<uploadUrlPrefix>/<customer_id>/<item_id>` per FR-62 (`[D-074]` Variant A); example: `https://hilda-proxy.corp/upload/MMK/12345`; same `<customer_id>` + `<item_id>` discipline as View Documents above (per FR-5); visible when `item_type ≠ Confirmation` AND `delivery_state ∈ {DocumentReceived, UnderPMReview, ReadyForSubmission, SubmittedToCustomer}` per FR-62 rewrite 2026-06-12 (or in `Open` state when effective `tracking_enabled` flag is `False` per FR-81 no-tracking-TG fallback ingest path); TPM uploads via HILDA-rendered server-side form (not SP-page-JS)
  - **Mark Closed** (PM/TPM; per FR-78 default work-item + FR-80 `no_customer_upload` items) — SP-side write to `delivery_state = Closed` per FR-78 / FR-80 enablement gates
  - **Clear Triage Flag** (PM/TPM; visible only when `manual_triage_required = true` per FR-12 I4) — SP-side write to `manual_triage_required = false`; HILDA logs `action_type = manual_triage_cleared`
  - **Confirm Carrier Acceptance** (PM/TPM; per FR-7 path (i) Closed manual override; visible when `delivery_state = SubmittedToCustomer`) — SP-side write to `delivery_state = Closed`; HILDA logs `action_type = manual_carrier_acceptance`; fires `MilestoneAllClosed` (FR-28) → `MilestoneStorageCleanup` (FR-76) if last gating item closed
  - **Resume from Delayed / Resume from Blocked** (PM/TPM; per FR-7 I2 exit path (b); visible when `delivery_state ∈ {Delayed, Blocked}` AND `prior_delivery_state` non-null) — SP-side write to `delivery_state = <prior_delivery_state value>`; HILDA atomically clears `prior_delivery_state` in same writeback; re-arms FR-11 `DeadlineProximity`
  - **Pause/Resume Item Rules** + **Pause All/Resume All** + **Trigger Action** dropdown (per FR-31) — SP-side write to `rules_paused_at` / `manual_action_*` columns per the rule control panel spec
  - **Detailed button specs**: see `docs/sp_ui_engineer/SP_UI_button_actions.md` Part 1 — Write Actions; FR-56 (c) is the requirements-level enumeration. SP UI engineer's doc is the authoritative implementation spec (rows have field-write contracts, enablement conditions, HILDA-side cascade descriptions, idempotency notes, and FR cross-refs per button).
  - FR-87 step (A)/(B)/(C) TPM-resolution buttons + FR-47 resolution-path picker are NOT in the SP milestone view — they live inline on document rows in HILDA's rendered tab per FR-87 / FR-47 / `[D-074]` Variant A (HILDA-rendered HTML at `hilda-proxy.corp/docs/<customer_id>/<item_id>`).

  **(d) Rendering surface split — SP-side vs HILDA-side per `[D-074]` 2026-06-12** (locked 2026-06-12; supersedes original FR-56 wording that conflated the two): the SP milestone view renders only what's listed in (a)/(b)/(c) above — per-row data columns + button affordances. **Document section, PM Review section, parser_result / llm_review_findings detail, FR-87 resolution buttons, FR-47 resolution-path picker** are ALL rendered by HILDA's `dashboard` module server-side HTML at `https://hilda-proxy.corp/docs/<customer_id>/<item_id>` per FR-57 / FR-59 / FR-60 / FR-47 / `[D-074]`; SP UI does NOT render these. TPM clicks the per-row **View Documents** link-out anchor (when `item_type ≠ Confirmation` per FR-58) → browser opens new HILDA tab → HILDA renders the full document section including PM Review section (when item state = `UnderPMReview`) + parser_result tables (FR-60) + llm_review_findings (FR-60) + inline FR-87 buttons (when document is in staged/unrouted state). Items where `item_type = Confirmation` show **no View Documents link-out** in the SP milestone view per FR-58 (Confirmation items have no documents).

  **(e) Milestone-level action buttons in SP top bar** (separate from per-row buttons):
  - **Start Collection** (PM/TPM-clickable; enabled when `milestone_collection_started_at IS NULL` on the Milestones row per FR-6 lock) → SP writes `milestone_collection_started_at = <now>` to the single Milestones SP list row for the milestone (single-row write per `[D-083]` 2-list architecture) → SP-alert per `[D-047]` → HILDA triggers FR-8 (PLM issue creation per `(device, milestone, owner_corp_id)` tuple per FR-5 + `[D-035]`) + FR-9 (initial outreach per `(device, milestone, owner_corp_id)` batch using preference rule)
  - **Submit to Carrier** (FR-63 enablement gate) → SP writes `milestone_submission_triggered_at = <now>` to the single Milestones SP list row (single-row write per `[D-083]`)
  - **Close All Items** (FR-64 enablement gate: all `milestone_gating=true` items in `{SubmittedToCustomer, Closed}`; at least one in `SubmittedToCustomer`) → SP writes `closed_all_items_triggered_at = <now>` to the single Milestones SP list row (single-row write per `[D-083]`)
  - **Refresh** (PM/TPM-clickable; rate-limited HILDA-side) → see (f) below

  **(f) On-demand soft refresh** (per `[D-074]` SP-side field write + SP-alert pattern): the milestone view's **Refresh** button writes `refresh_requested_at = <now>` to the single Milestones SP list row (single-row write per `[D-083]`); the SP-alert per `[D-047]` / FR-84 fires; `email_service.sp_alert_parser` routes the alert to a `refresh_milestone` handler; HILDA enqueues a soft-poll Celery task that checks Email (FR-23), PLM (FR-26), and NSD (FR-55) for new items since their respective `last_poll_timestamp`; rate-limited HILDA-side (configurable, default 5 min per milestone — prevents hammering); deduplicated HILDA-side (if a poll task for this milestone is already in-progress, the alert is acknowledged and no new task is enqueued). The milestone view displays a **Last updated: X min ago** timestamp indicator derived from SP's built-in `Modified` field on DeliveryItem rows (post-2026-06-12 — `last_updated` custom column was dropped per SP UI engineer 2026-06-10 review; use SP system column instead). After soft-poll completes, HILDA writes back SP List row updates via REST per `[D-064]`; the SP web part picks up the updates on next page refresh or **focus-aware refresh** (SP UI engineer's choice per STATUS line 290 — on tab/window focus-gain, web part re-fetches list data from SP, NOT from HILDA). **No SP-page-JS polling of HILDA endpoints** — JS-side polling of `hilda.corp/status/*` is structurally closed per `[D-074]` and is explicitly NOT part of this requirement.

  **(g) Ph-2 deferred per-item actions** `[Ph-2]`: (1) **Owner version selection per FR-66** — for items with multiple revisions of any `doc_id_slug`, owner selects the final revision via corp messenger workflow per FR-66 (`TriggerVersionSelection` fires per FR-28); HILDA writes `is_final = true` on the selected revision's `DocumentIndexRow` atomically with `is_final = false` on all others for that `doc_id_slug`. (2) **TPM `is_final` override in HILDA-rendered document section per `[D-074]`** — the PM Review section (HILDA-rendered HTML, NOT SP UI) allows TPM to override the owner-selected revision before approving — selecting a different revision sets `is_final = true` on it and `is_final = false` on all others for that `doc_id_slug`. (3) **Close Item SP-side button** — enables item's assigned owner to self-close directly from the SP UI (per FR-7 `[Ph-2]` enum).

  ~~Original FR-56 wording (struck 2026-06-12 per `[D-074]` rendering-surface split — pre-D-074 paragraph conflated SP-side rendering and HILDA-side rendering; the document section, PM Review section, parser_result, and llm_review_findings are all HILDA-rendered per FR-59/FR-60, not SP-rendered): items where `item_type = Confirmation` show no document section; all other item types show a document section populated via `actual_item_info` (FR-57); items in `UnderPMReview` state display a PM Review section showing all received documents with their parser_result and llm_review_findings (from the HILDA document enumeration API per FR-57)...~~ Rendering responsibility moved from SP to HILDA `dashboard` module per `[D-074]`; SP web part emits link-out anchors only.

  ~~Original wording (struck 2026-06-12 per `[D-074]`): on each page load of the HILDA milestone-view web part, the web part calls `POST /milestone/{id}/refresh`; HILDA enqueues a soft-poll Celery task...; after soft-poll completes, the SP web part detects updated SP List rows by polling the HILDA status endpoint at 10 s intervals until the task completes.~~ Both the `POST /milestone/{id}/refresh` and the 10s status polling were SP-page-JS calls to HILDA, blocked by corp Content Security Policy / network ACL. Mechanism shifted to SP-side field write + SP-alert per `[D-074]`.

- **FR-57** `[Ph-1]` — **REWRITTEN 2026-06-12 — cleanup of stale primary/secondary association model + outdated PK references per storage 2026-06-07 file-centric refactor + `[D-072]` partial unique index + FR-86 4-path matrix + `[D-074]` Variant A**. `actual_item_info` on `DeliveryItem` stores the PLM issue URL for the `(device, milestone, owner)` tuple, constructed from `plm_id` by the IssueTracker adapter; set on first document arrival per any ingest channel — FR-52 (email), FR-55 (NSD), FR-26 (PLM poll), or FR-62 `[Ph-2]` (HILDA dashboard upload) — and stable thereafter; this URL is the direct link to the PLM issue where all owner deliverables for this `(device, milestone, owner)` tuple are attached.

  **Document section rendering**: HILDA's `dashboard` module server-side-renders the document section as HTML at `https://hilda-proxy.corp/docs/<customer_id>/<item_id>` per `[D-074]` (2026-06-12) — the SP web part emits a per-item link-out anchor (`<a href target="_blank">`); the browser does the top-level GET; HILDA queries its document index (populated at ingest time per FR-52 email, FR-55 NSD, FR-26 PLM poll, and FR-62 `[Ph-2]` HILDA dashboard upload) and Jinja-renders the section as HTML. **URL token vs. storage identity**: `<customer_id>/<item_id>` is the URL token (`<customer_id>` selects the SP list `Deliverables_<customer_id>`; `<item_id>` = SP system `ID` column, per-list unique only per FR-5). `delivery_item_id` referenced elsewhere in this FR (and FR-61 token contract, FR-87 storage APIs) is the HILDA-internal storage identity in `DocumentItemAssociation` — it composes from `(customer_id, item_id)` and is opaque to URL consumers; SP web parts and the browser see only `<customer_id>/<item_id>`. The **internal data shape** fed to the renderer is `[{doc_type, doc_id_slug, rev_number, original_filename, download_url, parser_result, llm_review_findings, inferred_tg_name, nsd_path_type, is_final}]` (extended 2026-06-09 per `[D-060]` impl note + `is_final` added 2026-06-16 per FR-7 Ph-2 fork + FR-66 + FR-46 I9). **`download_url` is computed at HTML-render time per `[D-057]` + FR-61 — NOT persisted on `DocumentIndexRow`**. Reason: the file's NSD location is **transient** — a document moves between the FR-86 4 NSD paths during its lifecycle (ingest landing → FR-87 TPM resolution → FR-83 reassignment can each move the file). Persisting a URL would require updating it on every move; computing at runtime via `make_download_token(file_hash, delivery_item_id)` per storage MODULE.md automatically resolves to the CURRENT location via `DocumentItemAssociation.local_nsd_path` (which IS persisted). Additional benefits: 300 s token TTL per NFR-16 reduces leak risk; per-(file, item) token enables tracking; expired tokens force fresh render rather than exposing stale URLs. Same shape is available via `Accept: application/json` for HILDA-internal admin tools but **is not consumed by SP UI** (SP-page-JS cross-origin XHR to HILDA is blocked by corp policy per FR-84 + `[D-074]`).

  **Document index identity model** (revised 2026-06-12 per storage 2026-06-07 file-centric refactor + `[D-072]`): primary key is `file_hash` (SHA-256 per `[D-039]` Step 0); secondary **partial unique index** `(milestone_id, doc_id_slug, rev_number) WHERE doc_id_slug IS NOT NULL AND rev_number IS NOT NULL` per `[D-072]` preserves the FR-57 lookup contract for resolved rows. Per-item association lives on the `DocumentItemAssociation` M:M table with composite PK `(file_hash, delivery_item_id)` — **symmetric M:M, no primary/secondary distinction** (supersedes the prior "primary item association / highest-tag-count match" model dropped during 2026-06-07 refactor). One physical file = one `DocumentIndexRow` + N `DocumentItemAssociation` rows (one per associated item). **The same file_hash may occupy multiple NSD paths simultaneously** — one physical copy per associated item per `[D-055]`. PLM fan-out is per-`(owner_corp_id, plm_id)` pair across associations per FR-79 + FR-52 I10 lock — one PLM upload per distinct `(owner_corp_id, plm_id)` target; **key is `owner_corp_id`** (corp identifier per FR-88), NOT email — per FR-5 plm_id grouping (corp_id is the stable identity; emails can vary per preference rule). **Multi-scope behavior** (the fan-out key naturally handles all 3 scenarios where same owner spans multiple scopes): **(a) one owner across multiple TGs within the SAME `(device, milestone)` tuple** — all those items share ONE `plm_id` per FR-5 (TG is sub-tuple within `(device, milestone, owner)`) → ONE PLM upload covers all TGs' items for that owner. **(b) one owner across DIFFERENT milestones for the same device** — different `plm_id` per milestone per FR-8 step 2 → ONE PLM upload PER milestone. **(c) one owner across DIFFERENT devices for the same customer** — different `plm_id` per `(device, milestone)` tuple per FR-5 → ONE PLM upload PER (device, milestone) tuple.

  **Field semantics**: `original_filename` is the filename exactly as received from the ingest channel — preserved without transformation; `doc_id_slug` is the slugified derivative of the first-received filename for that `(milestone_id, doc_type)` pair and is stable across revisions (milestone-scoped per the file-centric refactor; supersedes the prior per-delivery_item scoping); `rev_number` is 1 for new documents, ≥2 for revisions per FR-17 — both `doc_id_slug` and `rev_number` are NULL while the file is in any of the 3 staged NSD paths per FR-86 (`staged_not_classified` / `staged_not_revision` / `unrouted` — these are the FR-86 4-path-matrix enum values; the corresponding NSD subdirectory for `staged_not_revision` is named `_staged_revision/` per FR-13 area (3) underscore-prefix convention) and are atomically populated together when the file moves to the `classified` path per `[D-072]` staged-fill lifecycle. `download_url` is a HILDA-mediated link per FR-61 that **path-agnostically** resolves to whichever of the 4 FR-86 NSD paths the file is currently at via `DocumentItemAssociation.local_nsd_path` (corrects the prior wording which assumed classified-path-only resolution). `parser_result` and `llm_review_findings` are **null** for revisions not yet reviewed; on persistent FR-16 parser failure `parser_result` is populated with `status = parser_failed` + error code (`TST-W001` malformed structure / `TST-W002` profile mismatch / etc.) + raw text excerpt per FR-16 I6; on persistent FR-53 LLM review failure `llm_review_findings` may remain null but the work-item row's `review_status` is set to `failed` per FR-53 I8 (4th enum value `pending / complete / not_required / failed`).

  **Phase scope**: `[Ph-1]` returns one entry per `(doc_type, doc_id_slug)` — always `rev1`, as only one revision exists per document in Ph-1 per FR-7 single-revision fork; `[Ph-2]` default (no query parameter): returns one entry per `(doc_type, doc_id_slug)` — **the `is_final = true` revision if FR-66 owner version selection (or TPM override per FR-56 (g)) has completed for that `doc_id_slug`; otherwise the latest revision by `rev_number` as a pre-selection display fallback** — used by FR-59 list view; `[Ph-2]` with `all_revisions=true`: returns all revision rows per `(doc_type, doc_id_slug)` ordered by `rev_number` ascending — used by FR-60 expandable history view.

- **FR-58** `[Ph-1]` — **`item_type` discriminator for document-carrying behavior** (per FR-7 4-value enum lock per `[D-053]`): `item_type ∈ {Confirmation, TEST_TECH_WAIVER_REPORT, COMPLIANCE_CERTIFICATION_RELEASE_NOTES, Default}`. **Behavior per item_type**: **(a) Confirmation** — owner reply closes the item; no artifact deliverable. **No document section** in HILDA-rendered tab, **no document routing** (FR-52 not invoked), **no FR-16 parser** (no docs), **no FR-53 LLM review** (`review_required = false` always and **non-editable** — TPM cannot set `review_required = true` on a Confirmation item per FR-7 I5 lock). **No View Documents link-out anchor** in SP milestone view (per FR-56 (c) gating). Owner-done signal varies by modality — see CustomerJIRA-only lock below. **(b) TEST_TECH_WAIVER_REPORT** — receives any of `{test_report, tech_report, waiver}` doc_types per FR-7 alignment invariant. **Document routing** via FR-52 / FR-55 / FR-26 / FR-62 `[Ph-2]`. **`review_required` default = `true`** (TPM-editable per FR-7 I5 + FR-14). **FR-16 parser** fires when an aligned `test_report` document lands `classified` per FR-86. **FR-53 LLM review** fires for `{test_report, tech_report, waiver}` doc_types when `review_required = true`. Document display per FR-59 / FR-60 in HILDA-rendered tab per `[D-074]`. **(c) COMPLIANCE_CERTIFICATION_RELEASE_NOTES** — receives `compliance_certification_release_notes` doc_type per FR-7 alignment invariant. **Document routing** via FR-52 / FR-55 / FR-26 / FR-62 `[Ph-2]`. **`review_required` default = `false`** (TPM-editable but typically left false per FR-7 I5 — no LLM review for `compliance_certification_release_notes` per FR-53 downstream-triggering rule). **No FR-16 parser** (parser fires only for `doc_type = test_report`; a `test_report` doc on a COMPLIANCE_CERTIFICATION_RELEASE_NOTES item lands on the `staged_not_classified` path per FR-86 alignment invariant — no parser fires until TPM resolves via FR-87). Document display per FR-59 / FR-60. **(d) Default** (per FR-78) — per-milestone auto-instantiated work-item for unrouted docs. Receives **any of the 5 doc_types** per FR-7 alignment invariant (the only item_type without doc_type restrictions). **`review_required` default = `false`** per FR-7 I5 (review pending until TPM reassignment via FR-83 + FR-87). **Mark Closed manual path** per FR-78 — TPM clicks "Mark Closed" after all unrouted docs have been reassigned to real work-items via FR-83; HILDA enforces the no-remaining-association guard. Document display per FR-59 / FR-60 + FR-87 resolution buttons inline on document rows per `[D-074]`. **`doc_count = 0` skip rule (per FR-7 I8)**: Confirmation items always have `doc_count = 0` and therefore skip `DocumentReceived` + `OwnerClosed` entirely per the FR-7 I8 unified skip rule (owner-done confirmation from `OutreachSent` advances directly to `UnderPMReview`). **Non-Confirmation items with `doc_count = 0`** (template-author opt-in for waiver-only / tech-only cases) follow the same skip path — docs may still arrive (and are stored + reviewed per `review_required`) but do NOT gate state advancement. **CustomerJIRA-only items locked to Confirmation per FR-7 I4 + FR-9 lock**: items with `tracking_modality = [CustomerJIRA]` alone (no other modality) MUST have `item_type = Confirmation` — status-only modality is meaningless for items expecting documents. State machine (CORRECTED 2026-06-17 per FR-25 (b) lock cascade): `Open → OutreachSent` (FR-8 initial outreach email DOES fire for CustomerJIRA-only items per FR-25 (b) lock — body tailored to JIRA modality: "please review open JIRA tickets at `<customer_jira_url>` and reply to confirm milestone completion"; recipient is role-collapsed owner-PM per FR-25 (b)); `OutreachSent → UnderPMReview` (close-intent reply email per FR-12 + FR-24 — skipping `DocumentReceived` + `OwnerClosed` per FR-7 I8 Confirmation skip rule). **HILDA-tab access for non-Confirmation items**: TPM reaches the HILDA-rendered document section per FR-59 / FR-60 / FR-61 via the per-item View Documents link-out anchor in SP UI (per FR-56 (c)) → top-level browser navigation to HILDA-rendered HTML at `hilda-proxy.corp/docs/<customer_id>/<item_id>` per `[D-074]` Variant A.

- **FR-59** `[Ph-1]` — **REWRITTEN 2026-06-12 per `[D-074]` (Variant A SP↔HILDA integration)**. HILDA's `dashboard` module **server-side-renders the document section as HTML** at `https://hilda-proxy.corp/docs/<customer_id>/<item_id>` (per FR-57). The SP milestone view (FR-56) emits a per-item `<a href="https://hilda-proxy.corp/docs/<customer_id>/<item_id>" target="_blank">View Documents</a>` link-out anchor for items where `item_type ≠ Confirmation`; the browser does a top-level GET to HILDA; HILDA renders one row per document grouped by `doc_type` (5-value per `[D-053]`: `test_report` / `tech_report` / `waiver` / `compliance_certification_release_notes` / `unresolved`); documents with `doc_type = unresolved` (**transient state per FR-7 I7** — only at ingest before TPM resolution via FR-87 step (B)) appear under an **`unresolved` group header** with the inline FR-87 step (B) Resolve doc_type picker readily available for TPM resolution; once TPM resolves the doc_type, the doc moves to its resolved-doc_type group (or to the `staged_not_revision` group if revision determination is still pending in Ph-2); each row shows `doc_type`, `doc_id_slug` (human-readable label; `—` when null per FR-86 staged-fill), `rev_number` (or `—` when null), `original_filename` (filename as received for that revision), `parser_result` summary (test_report only), `llm_review_findings` summary (test/tech/waiver only), `inferred_tg_name` (when `nsd_path_type = unrouted`, helps TPM group default-work-item docs by channel-resolved TG before FR-87 step (A)), `nsd_path_type` badge (one of `classified` / `staged_not_classified` / `staged_not_revision` / `unrouted` per FR-86 + `[D-055]`), and a HILDA-mediated download link (FR-61) embedded as `<a href>` to `/dl/<scoped_token>` (TPM clicks → browser GETs `/dl/...` direct from HILDA → file streams). Each row also surfaces **failure-state badges** when applicable: (a) **`parser_failed` badge** when `parser_result.status = parser_failed` (per FR-16 I6) — displays error code (e.g., `TST-W001` malformed structure / `TST-W002` profile mismatch) + raw text excerpt link for TPM triage; (b) **`review_failed` badge** when the work-item row's `review_status = failed` (per FR-53 I8) — displays error code (e.g., `LLM-W001` LLM gateway timeout / `LLM-W002` low-confidence) and indicates the parser result may still be available for partial review. **View in PLM** link is NOT rendered in the HILDA-tab document section (moved to SP per-row button only per FR-56 column model 2026-06-12 — per-item PLM URL is item-level, not per-document; lives in SP milestone view where TPMs already have item context). TPM accesses PLM directly from the SP milestone view's per-row View in PLM button using `actual_item_info` (the PLM issue URL per FR-57); HILDA tab focuses on per-document view, downloads, and FR-87 resolution. FR-87 step (A)/(B)/(C) buttons surface inline on staged/unrouted document rows per `[D-074]` — same-origin form POST to dashboard endpoints (HILDA-tab-internal, no cross-origin issue). **FR-47 resolution-path picker `[Ph-2]`** also surfaces inline in the document section per FR-46 I9 + FR-47 — for `interim` test reports (per FR-46 final|interim rule), each `failed` test case without a `waiver_ref` gets a resolution-path picker (`resolution_path ∈ {fix_pre_launch, tech_report, waiver}`); PM selects per FR-47. Same-origin form POST to dashboard endpoints — same pattern as FR-87 step (A)/(B)/(C). In Ph-1 each document has exactly one version at `rev1/` regardless of ingest channel — NSD-dropped files are processed from the milestone's ingress folder `\\NSD_X\<carrier>\<device>\<milestone>\<Folder-N>\` per FR-13 (flat per-TG folder structure — NOT a per-item `inbound/`) and written to `<item_path_id>/<doc_type_slug>/<doc_id_slug>/rev1/` in the NSD1 internal tree by HILDA before they appear in the document section; `[Ph-2]` when multiple revisions exist, the list view shows **the `is_final = true` revision** if FR-66 owner version selection (or TPM override per FR-56 (g)) has completed for that `doc_id_slug`; otherwise the latest revision by `rev_number` as a pre-selection display fallback per FR-57 phase scope (the FR-60 expandable history view shows all revisions ordered ascending).

  ~~Original wording (struck 2026-06-12 per `[D-074]`): The SharePoint UI document section for each applicable DeliveryItem calls the HILDA document enumeration API (FR-57) and renders one row per document grouped by `doc_type`...~~ Rendering responsibility moved from SP UI to HILDA `dashboard` module per `[D-074]`; SP-page-JS cross-origin XHR to HILDA is blocked by corp policy (Content Security Policy / network ACL — tested + confirmed 2026-06-12); top-level browser navigation is the structurally-available channel.

- **FR-60** `[Ph-1]` — **REWRITTEN 2026-06-12 per `[D-074]`**. **HILDA's `dashboard` module displays review results alongside each document row in the rendered document section** (FR-59): **(a) rule-based parser output from FR-16** — **per-test-case** status grid (showing each `(test_case_id, status, [waiver_ref])` tuple per FR-16) AND `final | interim | not_classified` classification per FR-46 — for `test_report` documents (`not_classified` per FR-46 I10 lock when parser persistently failed — see Parser-failure display below). **Parser-failure display**: when `parser_result.status = parser_failed` (per FR-16 I6 lock), the per-test-case status grid is replaced by an inline error display showing the FR-16 error code (`TST-W001` malformed structure / `TST-W002` profile mismatch / etc.) + a link to the raw text excerpt for TPM triage; `final | interim` classification is replaced by `not_classified` per FR-46 I10. Row surfaces a **`parser_failed` badge** (per FR-59 I4 lock). FR-53 LLM review still fires when `review_required = true` (degraded-mode review independent of parser success per FR-16 I6 lock); LLM findings display independently of parser state. **(b) LLM quality review findings from FR-53** for `test_report` / `tech_report` / `waiver` documents — when `review_required = false` for the item, the `llm_review_findings` column displays `Review not required` and `review_status` shows `not_required`; when `review_required = true` and review is pending, `review_status` shows `pending`; when complete, `review_status` shows `complete` with findings; when review **persistently failed** (per FR-53 I8 lock), `review_status` shows `failed` with the failure error code (e.g., `LLM-W001` LLM gateway timeout / `LLM-W002` low-confidence) displayed inline; the `llm_review_findings` column shows `Review failed — see HildaOpsAlert` and the row surfaces a **`review_failed` badge** (per FR-59 I4 lock) — partial review may still be available from the FR-16 parser output. **Both result sets** are stored in the document index per revision (`parser_result` and `llm_review_findings` fields, keyed by `file_hash` per `[D-055]` file-centric storage model) and embedded in the HILDA-rendered HTML per FR-57; `[Ph-1]` `rev1` review results are rendered inline on each document row — only one revision exists per document in Ph-1; `[Ph-2]` each document row is expandable to show all revisions with their individual `parser_result` and `llm_review_findings`, enabling the PM to track the improvement trajectory across revisions (e.g., rev1: interim — 3 failures; rev2: final — all passed). **FR-47 resolution-path picker integration** `[Ph-2]`: for `interim` test reports per FR-46 final|interim rule, each `failed` test case in the per-test-case status grid that lacks a `waiver_ref` is rendered with the **FR-47 resolution-path picker inline** (per FR-46 I9 + FR-47 + FR-59 I3 lock) — `resolution_path ∈ {fix_pre_launch, tech_report, waiver}`. PM selects per FR-47 via same-origin form POST to dashboard endpoints. `final` test reports show no picker (no unresolved failures). `failed` test cases WITH `waiver_ref` are reclassified as `waived` per FR-46 reclassification rule and show as `waived` in the grid with no picker. Rendering responsibility moved from SP UI to HILDA `dashboard` module per `[D-074]` (parallel to FR-59 rewrite).

- **FR-61** `[Ph-1]` — **REWRITTEN 2026-06-12 per `[D-074]` + FR-86 4-path alignment**. Each document row in HILDA's rendered document section (per FR-59) provides a HILDA-mediated download link (`https://hilda-proxy.corp/dl/<scoped_token>`) authenticated via on-prem AD per NFR-16 — Ph-1 grants access to any authenticated corp AD user; per-DeliveryItem ACL restricting access to assigned PMs only is deferred (DEF-18); **the link resolves to the file's current NSD location per `storage.DocumentItemAssociation.local_nsd_path` regardless of which of the 4 FR-86 path types** the file currently occupies (`classified` / `staged_not_classified` / `staged_not_revision` / `unrouted`) — **the download mechanism is path-type agnostic** (corrects original wording which referenced "NSD classified path" only; superseded by FR-86 4-path matrix landed 2026-06-09). The token encodes `(file_hash, delivery_item_id)` per storage MODULE.md `make_download_token`; token resolution is **stable across FR-87 step (B) doc_type resolution and step (C) revision resolution** (file_hash + delivery_item_id unchanged across those resolutions; only the NSD path changes — token does fresh path lookup at download time); token is **invalidated by FR-83 work-item reassignment** (delivery_item_id changes; old association deleted) **and by 300s TTL expiry** — both surface as DSH-E002 (friendly "Link expired — please refresh" page rendered by dashboard, not HTTP 500). HILDA reads the document from the NSD and streams it via HTTP response with **content-type-conditional disposition**: `Content-Disposition: inline` for browser-renderable types (PDF, plain text, images — these display in the user's browser tab); `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename=<original_filename>` for Office documents (Excel `.xlsx`/`.xls`/`.xlsm`, Word `.docx`/`.doc`, PowerPoint `.pptx`/`.ppt`) and other binary types — these download to the user's local Downloads folder (distinct from FR-18 batch submission assembly which downloads to HILDA PC for carrier upload); in Ph-1 each document has exactly one revision; `[Ph-2]` in the FR-59 list view the download link resolves to the **`is_final = true` revision** if FR-66 owner version selection (or TPM override per FR-56 (g)) has completed for that `doc_id_slug`; otherwise the latest revision by `rev_number` as a pre-selection display fallback per FR-57 phase scope (consistent with FR-59 I2 lock); in the FR-60 expandable history each revision row carries its own download link resolving to that specific `rev_number`, making all revisions independently downloadable; download is available only for items where `item_type ≠ Confirmation`. **Post-cleanup behavior** (per FR-76 MilestoneStorageCleanup): once all `milestone_gating = true` items + the default work-item per FR-78 reach `Closed` AND FR-76 triggers (per FR-64 Close All Items + FR-78 Mark Closed cascade), the NSD `internal/<carrier>/<device>/<milestone>/` subtree is deleted per FR-76 cleanup scope. Download tokens issued before cleanup resolve to deleted paths → HILDA detects missing file at `make_download_token` resolution time and returns **`DSH-E003` "Document no longer available — milestone was closed and storage cleaned per FR-76"** (friendly dashboard page, not HTTP 404). TPM is warned of this irrevocability in the Close All Items button prompt per FR-64 + SP_UI_button_actions.md (Close All Items button spec). **NSD-unreachable failure** (transient): if HILDA cannot reach NSD at download time (SMB mount unreachable, network partition), HILDA returns **`DSH-E004` "Document temporarily unavailable — please retry"** with HTTP 503 + `Retry-After` header; logged to `HildaOpsAlert` per FR-75; retry per NFR-10 backoff. Distinct from `DSH-E003` post-cleanup case (permanent) — `DSH-E004` is transient and retry-eligible. **Download tokens are generated by HILDA at HTML-render time** and embedded as `<a href>` in the response; tokens never cross the SP↔HILDA boundary; SP UI never sees them.

- **FR-62** `[Ph-2]` — **REWRITTEN 2026-06-12 per `[D-074]`**. The PM/TPM document upload surface is **rendered by HILDA's `dashboard` module** as a server-side form at `https://hilda-proxy.corp/upload/<customer_id>/<item_id>` per `[D-074]` (not by SP UI — SP-page-JS cross-origin POST to HILDA is blocked by corp policy). SP web part emits a per-item **Upload Document** link-out anchor for items where `item_type ≠ Confirmation` AND `delivery_state ∈ {DocumentReceived, UnderPMReview, ReadyForSubmission, SubmittedToCustomer}` (ReadyForSubmission added 2026-06-12 — covers TPM's late-doc upload before the next FR-63 Submit fires: supplementary test report, customer-induced revision, > doc_count overflow, or any miscellaneous artifact the TPM realizes is missing after PM approval but before Submit) AND additionally in `Open` state when the effective `tracking_enabled` flag is `False` (per FR-81 — no-tracking TGs depend on FR-62 as their sole functional ingest path) OR when `tracking_modality` includes `SPUI` per FR-9 silent-outreach rule (SPUI items have no HILDA-initiated outreach; FR-62 is the TPM-side upload path; on upload from Open state, FR-7 `AttachmentReceived` advances `delivery_state` to `DocumentReceived`); TPM clicks → browser opens HILDA tab → HILDA renders an upload form (file picker + `doc_type` dropdown + New/Revision radio); item is fixed (URL-encoded in the page route); the form additionally prompts the PM/TPM to declare: **New document** or **Revision of existing** — if **Revision**, a dropdown lists all existing `doc_id_slug` entries for the selected `(delivery_item_id, doc_type)` from the HILDA document index and the PM/TPM selects which document is being revised; this explicit PM/TPM declaration replaces `[D-039]` classification entirely for this ingest path — no hash comparison, no LLM Tier-2 call, no `staged/` holding; on form submit, browser does **same-origin** multipart POST to `/upload/<customer_id>/<item_id>` (no cross-origin issue — TPM is already in HILDA tab); HILDA determines `rev_number` from the declaration: **New document** → `rev1/`; **Revision** → `revN/` where N = (current max `rev_number` for the selected `doc_id_slug`) + 1; HILDA writes the document file to `<NSD_root>/internal/<carrier>/<device>/<milestone>/<tg>/<item>/<doc_type>/<doc_id_slug>/revN/` per FR-86 (classified) path — direct write to the classified hierarchy (no `_staged_*/` intermediate state, since explicit PM/TPM declaration bypasses [D-039]'s two-tier flow per FR-85 stage 3); **PLM upload is deferred per FR-13's Ph-2 rule** (FR-62 fires only in Ph-2) — the deferred upload fires post-dispatch via FR-68 sync verification, uploading only the `is_final = true` revision (anchors `[D-040]`); intermediate revisions are never uploaded to PLM. PLM upload is skipped entirely when `no_customer_upload = true` (FR-80). HILDA updates the HILDA document index with the standard ingest field set (`ingest_source = HILDA_DASHBOARD` per FR-13 rename locked 2026-06-13 — the actual ingest channel is HILDA-rendered form, not SP UI; supersedes the prior `SharePointUI` value), updates the DeliveryItem record in SharePoint via `[D-064]` REST writeback (`actual_item_info` is already set to the PLM issue URL per FR-57 from the item's first document arrival and does not change), and triggers review per FR-53; **`review_status` lifecycle on upload** (per FR-53 I14/I15 lock): if `review_required = true`, HILDA flips `review_status` from current value (which would have been `complete` for items in `ReadyForSubmission` / `SubmittedToCustomer`, since FR-7 OwnerClosed guard condition (2) required `complete` for those items to be there) back to `pending` atomically with the DocumentIndexRow insert — the newly-uploaded doc is unreviewed; FR-7's OwnerClosed guard condition (2) re-requires `complete` before any future OwnerClosed re-entry. The field stays `failed` if any prior doc had persistent LLM review failure — the new upload doesn't reset that until the prior failed doc is re-reviewed. **`actual_completion_date` discipline on upload-induced revert**: `actual_completion_date` is **NOT** cleared when an upload reverts `SubmittedToCustomer → UnderPMReview` or `ReadyForSubmission → UnderPMReview` — FR-15-extended semantics anchor the column to owner-completion (the date the owner finished delivering the doc set), not to PM-workflow state; PM re-review/re-approve workflow leaves the owner-completion date intact. after submit, HILDA redirects (303) the browser back to `GET /docs/<customer_id>/<item_id>` so TPM sees the newly uploaded doc in the document section; state transitions on upload: if `delivery_state = SubmittedToCustomer`, item reverts to `UnderPMReview` (the prior submission is now stale — TPM must re-review and re-approve before the item can be re-submitted via FR-63); **if `delivery_state = ReadyForSubmission`, item reverts to `UnderPMReview` (the prior PM approval is now stale — new doc content changes what was approved; PM must re-approve before next Submit fires per FR-63; matches the SubmittedToCustomer revert-on-upload pattern; **`pm_approval_at` + `pm_approval_pm_id` are cleared atomically by SP UI engineer's web part during the SP-side `delivery_state` revert write** per `[D-068]` impl note 2026-06-12 clearing discipline — write-owner is SP, not HILDA, parallel to the set-on-Approve discipline; HILDA receives the revert via SP-alert and does not write these fields)** — added 2026-06-12; clearing-write-owner clarified 2026-06-13; if `delivery_state = UnderPMReview`, item remains in `UnderPMReview` — TPM reviews the newly uploaded revision in the PM Review section and approves; if `delivery_state = DocumentReceived`, item remains in `DocumentReceived`. **UX boundary on upload** (parallel to FR-87 sync/async boundary per [D-076] 2026-06-12): **(1) during file upload** — browser displays its native upload progress bar (multipart form POST, no custom JS); page is in submitting state; typical 1-10s for 1-50MB files on corp LAN. **(2) sync server processing** (post-receipt): auth check + form validation (file size against configured max default 100MB, MIME type, doc_type Choice membership, item lookup, doc_id_slug existence for Revision); compute file_hash + idempotent dedup check (duplicate hash → form redisplay with info banner pointing to existing rev); NSD file write via aiofiles; `storage.add_document_index_row` + `storage.add_document_item_association` + `storage.log_communication` (`action_type='upload_via_dashboard'`); enqueue workflow_engine task for async tail; **303 redirect to `GET /docs/<customer_id>/<item_id>`**. Net latency target: <2s server-side after upload finishes; <3s including the small enqueue + redirect; **<12s total click-to-redirect** including network upload. **(3) sync errors surface as form-redisplay**: `DSH-E008` (file too large), `DSH-E009` (unsupported MIME type), `DSH-E010` (Revision doc_id_slug doesn't exist for (item, doc_type)), `STR-E0XX` (NSD write failure), `STR-E005` (cross-milestone association), `STR-E007` (expired upload-form token — surfaces when an FR-83 reassignment changes `<item_id>` between form render and form POST; forms hold a `(customer_id, item_id, item_version)` HMAC-signed nonce at render time per `[D-076]` sync surface discipline; FR-83 increments `item_version` to invalidate; form-redisplay shows "Item assignment changed — close this tab and reopen Upload Document from SP"); 404 (item not found — stale link); 401 (auth failure); duplicate file_hash is informational not error. **(4) success outcome on landing at `/docs/<customer_id>/<item_id>`**: top-of-page success banner "Uploaded `<original_filename>` as `<doc_id_slug>` rev `<N>`" (auto-dismiss 5s or click); new document row appears in the doc section under its `doc_type` group — **for `New document`** (first revision) the new row sets `is_final = true` on insert (single revision; FR-66 trivially picks it) and surfaces immediately in the FR-59 list view; **for `Revision` of existing**, the new row inserts with `is_final = null` and surfaces in **FR-60 expandable history** — the **FR-59 list view** continues to display the previous `is_final = true` revision until FR-66 owner version selection (or FR-56 (g) TPM override) reassigns `is_final` per FR-57 I7 lock; the success banner names the new `rev <N>` so TPM can expand the doc history (FR-60) to view/download it directly. If `review_required=true` a "LLM review pending" badge surfaces on the new row (clears when async tail completes); under FR-62's Ph-2 deferred-PLM-upload model no PLM badge appears until FR-68 post-dispatch sync verification. **(5) async-tail errors surface via three channels** identical to FR-87 [D-076]: (α) inline badge on document row on next `/docs/<customer_id>/<item_id>` visit (primary — FR-53 LLM review failure, `[D-064]` SP writeback failure); (β) top-of-page banner for recent FR-62 actions within N minutes (secondary); (γ) TPM email for stuck cases > N min unrecoverable (escalation). Standard error codes: `DSH-W003` (FR-62 async tail in progress; informational), `DSH-W004` (FR-62 async tail failed; surfaced as badge).

- **FR-63** `[Ph-1]` — **REWRITTEN 2026-06-17 — gating-aware enablement per FR-64 + FR-28 lock cascade; explicit category exclusions; default-work-item soft warn (Approach C); FR-62 revert blocker tooltip; first-time vs re-submission confirmation gate text**. The SharePoint milestone view (FR-56) presents a **Submit to Carrier** action available to PM and TPM. **Enablement** (gating-aware, parallel to FR-64 lock 2026-06-13): enabled when **all `milestone_gating = true` DeliveryItems** in the milestone are in `ReadyForSubmission` or `SubmittedToCustomer` state AND **at least one** `milestone_gating = true` item is in `ReadyForSubmission` (otherwise disabled — nothing to submit); items with `milestone_gating = false` do NOT block enablement and may remain in any state at click time. The `milestone_gating` flag is YAML-loaded per template and not TPM-editable (per FR-56 column model). **Excluded from the enablement check** regardless of `milestone_gating`: **(a) default work-item per FR-78** — its lifecycle is `Open → Closed` via FR-78 TPM Mark Closed (never traverses `ReadyForSubmission` / `SubmittedToCustomer`); does NOT gate Submit; **(b) items with `no_customer_upload = true`** per FR-80 — no carrier-submission path (`OwnerClosed → Closed` via TPM Mark Closed); **(c) items with `item_type = Confirmation`** per FR-7 — no documents to submit (Confirmation items are Yes/No attestations, not document deliveries). All three categories are also excluded from the assembled FR-18 dispatch package. **FR-62 revert blocker** (interaction with FR-62 upload-induced revert): if any `milestone_gating = true` item is in `UnderPMReview` (typically because an FR-62 upload reverted it from `SubmittedToCustomer` or `ReadyForSubmission`), Submit is disabled — the item must re-traverse PM approval per FR-56 (e) to reach `ReadyForSubmission` again; the disabled button's hover tooltip names the blocking items: *"Submit blocked: {N} gating items in `UnderPMReview` — PM must re-approve per FR-56 (e). Items: {list}"*. **Multi-round per-work-item delivery semantic**: the FR-18 dispatch is per-work-item — each work-item has its own dedicated carrier-portal folder; each Submit click delivers items currently in `ReadyForSubmission` and skips those already in `SubmittedToCustomer`; non-gating items deliver to carrier in whichever Submit round they reach `ReadyForSubmission` (Round-1 may deliver gating items A, B + non-gating item D; Round-2 may deliver non-gating item C once it reaches `ReadyForSubmission`; A, B, D are skipped in Round-2 — already submitted; per-work-item carrier folders make this cross-round pattern natural). **Confirmation gate** (NFR-5): on activation, HILDA presents a delta summary varying by submission round: **(i) first-time submission** (no items in the milestone are in `SubmittedToCustomer` at click time): *"Submitting {N} items to {carrier_name}. Package contents: {N} `ReadyForSubmission` items dispatched as individual files per FR-18 (zip never uploaded per `[D-054]`); LLM review attestations stay in HILDA (audit trail per FR-53 / FR-60); no milestone-level cover sheet is generated. This action is irreversible from HILDA's side — any subsequent upload requires re-approval and re-submission per FR-62 revert pattern."*; **(ii) re-submission** (one or more items previously `SubmittedToCustomer`): *"Re-submitting {N} items with updated documents. {M} previously-submitted items are unchanged and excluded from this package."*. **Default-work-item soft warn (Approach C — locked 2026-06-17)**: if the default work-item has any documents currently routed to it (`storage.DocumentItemAssociation` count for the default `delivery_item_id` per FR-78 > 0), the confirmation gate additionally surfaces a non-blocking advisory: *"⚠ {K} documents are currently in the unrouted bucket (default work-item per FR-78) — confirm you've triaged any docs intended for this milestone; remaining docs may be junk/noise and can be triaged later via FR-87 or left for FR-78 Mark Closed cleanup. They will NOT be included in this submission package — they remain assigned to the default work-item until you reassign them. (Rationale for soft warn: default work-item is the FR-52 step 5 safety net designed to absorb unrouted noise; treating its non-emptiness as a hard blocker would invert that design.)"* — does NOT block confirm; PM/TPM may still proceed. PM/TPM confirms; HILDA assembles the submission package from only the items currently in `ReadyForSubmission` (excluding the three categories above) and dispatches per FR-18 via the customer adapter; items already in `SubmittedToCustomer` are skipped and remain unchanged; on successful dispatch, the included items advance to `SubmittedToCustomer`. The event is recorded in `CommunicationLog` with `action_type: resubmission` when any items in the milestone were already `SubmittedToCustomer` at the time of this action, or `action_type: submission` for the initial full-milestone submission. The action is milestone-scoped — there is no per-item submission from the SharePoint UI.

- **FR-64** `[Ph-1]` — The SharePoint milestone view (FR-56) presents a **Close All Items** action available to PM and TPM. **Enablement**: enabled when ALL `milestone_gating = true` DeliveryItems in the milestone are in `SubmittedToCustomer` or `Closed` state AND at least one `milestone_gating = true` item is in `SubmittedToCustomer` — items with `milestone_gating = false` do NOT block enablement and may remain in any state at click time (ops/PM team decides which items are critical-for-closure at template creation by setting `milestone_gating=true`; the flag is YAML-loaded and NOT TPM-editable per FR-56 column model). On activation, HILDA closes items in **two parts** to keep the state machine consistent with `MilestoneAllClosed` (FR-28) gating-aware semantic: **(part 1 — gating cascade)** all `SubmittedToCustomer` items in the milestone are set to `Closed` in one operation (regardless of `milestone_gating` value — non-gating items in `SubmittedToCustomer` are also closed; the flag affects enablement only, not the closure action's scope); **(part 2 — non-gating auto-advance, added 2026-06-13)** all `milestone_gating=false` items that are NOT in `{SubmittedToCustomer, Closed}` at click time are auto-advanced to `Closed` as part of the same operation (logged with `action_type: bulk_close_non_gating` and PM/TPM attribution; distinct from part 1's `action_type: bulk_close`); this ensures `MilestoneAllClosed` can fire (all `milestone_gating=true` items are by definition `Closed` post-click; non-gating items are also `Closed` post-cascade — no "abandoned" non-Closed items remain). **Default work-item exclusion (FR-78)**: Default work-item is **excluded from both cascade parts** — Part 1 doesn't apply because the default work-item never reaches `SubmittedToCustomer` (its lifecycle is `Open → Closed` via FR-78 TPM Mark Closed only); Part 2 doesn't apply because it is implicitly `milestone_gating=true` (hardcoded at FR-78 instantiation, YAML override ignored). The default work-item reaches `Closed` exclusively via FR-78 TPM Mark Closed, which the TPM must trigger explicitly after Close All Items per the FR-78 closure guard ("no remaining `DocumentItemAssociation` rows for this default work-item" — i.e., all docs routed to default have been reassigned to real items via FR-83 first); by design the default work-item is the LAST item to reach `Closed` in the milestone, gating `MilestoneAllClosed` per FR-28 + `MilestoneStorageCleanup` eligibility per FR-76. **Part 2 force-advance state-machine implications** (cascade with FR-7 + FR-80 + FR-15-extended): when Part 2 force-advances non-gating items to `Closed` from earlier states, the natural close path is bypassed — **(a) Confirmation items** (`item_type = Confirmation` per FR-7) — the owner-Yes/No answer field stays `NULL`; CommunicationLog records `action_type: bulk_close_non_gating` with prior state + null-answer flag for audit traceability (owner never answered); **(b) `no_customer_upload = true` items** (per FR-80) — the `OwnerClosed` audit trail is bypassed; **(c) `actual_completion_date` discipline** (per FR-15-extended) — the column stays `NULL` for Part 2 force-Closed items; Part 2 administrative close is NOT a genuine owner-completion event; items reaching `Closed` via Part 2 have no real completion date (parallel to FR-62's no-clear-on-revert lock 2026-06-17 — `actual_completion_date` is anchored to genuine owner-completion, not administrative close). The Closed-via-bulk audit lives in `CommunicationLog` (`action_type: bulk_close_non_gating`) where the prior state + triggered_at + PM/TPM attribution are recorded; queries needing "when did this item leave the workflow" should use `CommunicationLog` timestamp, not `actual_completion_date`. This is admin-override behavior by design — TPM consciously chose to close all items via this bulk action and confirmed the NFR-5 gate. **Confirmation gate (NFR-5)**: on activation, HILDA presents a confirmation gate with text: *"Close All Items will set the milestone state machine to terminal: (a) `{N_part1}` `SubmittedToCustomer` items will be set to `Closed` (gating cascade); (b) `{N_part2}` non-gating items in earlier states will be auto-advanced to `Closed` (state-machine consistency cascade); (c) the default work-item must still be Marked Closed separately per FR-78 to trigger `MilestoneAllClosed`. Closing all items will permanently delete NSD storage for this milestone (per FR-76 cleanup) — download any needed documents before proceeding. Document download links (FR-61) will return `DSH-E003` after cleanup. Only close items after the carrier has confirmed technical acceptance of the milestone submission. This action is irreversible."* — PM/TPM must confirm before proceeding. **SP write target** (per `[D-083]` 2-list architecture): on click, SP UI engineer's web part performs two write groups in one SP transaction: **(i)** `closed_all_items_triggered_at = <now>` on the single Milestones SP list row for the milestone (one trigger write → one SP-alert per FR-84 Source B); **(ii)** per-item `delivery_state` transitions on the N affected `Deliverables_<customer_id>` rows (Part 1 gating-cascade items + Part 2 non-gating force-advance items; default work-item row is NOT touched). HILDA receives the Milestones-row alert as the milestone-scoped close-all trigger; per-item delivery_state changes route per FR-84 Source A (Deliverables routing) for state-machine effects. Each cascade is logged in `CommunicationLog` per the two action_types (`bulk_close` for Part 1, `bulk_close_non_gating` for Part 2) with PM/TPM attribution + prior state per row. No `[D-082]` cascade-dedup needed for the trigger write (single Milestones row write per `[D-083]`). **Relationship with FR-22 (deferred — DEF-20)**: Close All Items is the Ph-1 manual path until FR-22 automated `Closed` transition lands at Ph-2. When FR-22 is implemented, Close All Items REMAINS as an explicit TPM admin override (force-close for milestones where automated `Closed` conditions aren't met — e.g., non-gating items stuck in earlier states without owner action); FR-22 handles the happy path (all gating items reach `SubmittedToCustomer` → `Closed` automatically per submission-acknowledgement contract). The two coexist; Close All Items is not obsolete after FR-22.

  ~~Original wording (struck 2026-06-12 — `milestone_gating` semantic now functional): enabled when all DeliveryItems in the milestone are in `SubmittedToCustomer` or `Closed` state...~~ Original wording treated all items as gating regardless of the `milestone_gating` flag (which then carried no functional effect). Rewrite makes the flag meaningful: only `milestone_gating = true` items block Close All Items enablement; non-gating items can remain open without blocking milestone closure (covers cases like optional waivers, advisory items, etc. that ops/PM team decided are not critical-for-closure at template creation).

- **FR-65** `[Ph-1]` — **UPDATED 2026-06-17 — email-only modality lock; Delayed/Blocked semantics; Open→OutreachSent transition; HILDA-managed column-write spec via `[D-064]`; cadence-reset rule; Editor attribution; default-WI exclusion**. Each DeliveryItem row in the SharePoint milestone view (FR-56) presents a **Send Reminder** action available to PM and TPM. **Enabled when** `delivery_state ∉ {OwnerClosed, ReadyForSubmission, SubmittedToCustomer, Closed}` AND `item_type ≠ Default` (default work-item per FR-78 has no owner — system-reserved TG `_unrouted`; the SP UI engineer's web part suppresses the Send Reminder button when `item_type = Default`). The action is available at any time regardless of the scheduled FR-10 cadence (it can be triggered between automated reminder rungs or before any have fired). **Modality lock — email-only**: on activation, HILDA immediately dispatches a reminder to the item's owner **via email only** per FR-9 "always email" lead-in lock — **PLM is not a messaging platform** (it's a tracking/issue-management system; HILDA opens/updates PLM issues for tracking per FR-79 but never sends owner messages there); **NSD is ingest-only**; **SPUI is ingest-only**; **CorpMessenger is reserved for FR-11 escalations** (not used for ad-hoc Send Reminder). Email recipient resolution per FR-9 preference rule using owner's `owner_corp_usa_email` / `owner_corp_email` (per `[D-080]` owner email split) + `tg_email_group_alias` if set. **`Open` state → `OutreachSent` transition**: when TPM clicks Send Reminder while item is in `Open`, the item advances to `OutreachSent` atomically with the send (parallel to FR-8 automated outreach activation); this counts as the first outreach event for FR-10 cadence purposes; `last_owner_contacted` is set; `reminder_count` is initialized to 1 (NOT 0 — the manual action IS the first contact); per-channel polling activation per FR-8 step 2 fires for the email channel. **`Delayed/Blocked` semantics** (cascade with FR-7 prior_delivery_state + FR-12 lock): action is available; HILDA dispatches reminder; does NOT auto-exit Delayed/Blocked (item stays in admin state until TPM explicitly uses Resume from Delayed/Blocked per SP_UI_button_actions.md to restore `prior_delivery_state`); does NOT count toward FR-10 automated cadence (cadence remains paused per FR-12); does NOT flip `manual_triage_required` (TPM-initiated, not late-response trigger); `last_owner_contacted` IS updated per FR-15 — manual TPM ping is still a contact event. **Column writes — HILDA service-layer writes side-effect columns via `[D-064]` REST writeback after receiving SP-alert from the trigger write**: SP UI engineer's web part writes only the trigger column `DeliveryItems.last_reminder_triggered_at = <now>` + Editor on click (SP-side audit only — the trigger and the PM/TPM attribution); HILDA receives the SP-alert per `[D-047]` + FR-84, performs the email dispatch, then writes back via `[D-064]` per FR-15-extended hub discipline: **(a) `last_owner_contacted`** ← `<now>` per FR-15 (manual TPM ping IS a contact event); **(b) `reminder_count`** ← current + 1 (Send Reminder counts toward per-channel reminder tally — TPM ping shares the same counter as automated reminders so cadence escalation per FR-10 sees the full contact pressure); **(c) `last_owner_response_at`** unchanged (no response received); **(d) `manual_triage_required`** unchanged (TPM-initiated outreach is NOT a late-response trigger per FR-12 lock); **(e) `prior_delivery_state`** unchanged (Send Reminder during active states doesn't transition through Delayed/Blocked; during Delayed/Blocked it doesn't auto-exit). **Write-owner discipline**: trigger column `last_reminder_triggered_at` is SP-write-owned (SP UI engineer's web part); side-effect columns (`last_owner_contacted`, `reminder_count`, `delivery_state` transition for Open→OutreachSent) are HILDA-write-owned via `[D-064]` REST writeback — parallel to FR-14 / FR-12 / FR-15-extended write-ownership pattern. On dispatch failure: HILDA logs to `HildaOpsAlert` per FR-75; `last_owner_contacted` stays unchanged; `reminder_count` stays unchanged; HILDA may retry per NFR-10 backoff. **Interaction with FR-10 automated escalation cadence**: TPM Send Reminder **resets the FR-10 cadence clock for that item** — the next automated reminder is rescheduled to fire `(current_rung_threshold)` days from the manual send timestamp (NOT from the prior auto-reminder). This prevents duplicate near-simultaneous owner contact (manual TPM ping at T=0 and auto reminder at T=+1 day would feel like spam to owner); preserves cadence escalation discipline (rung doesn't reset to 1 — the manual ping advances on the current rung, since `reminder_count` increments per (b) above). Combined-message rule per FR-11 still applies if subsequent multi-channel send overlaps. **Attribution** (per FR-14 + FR-87 attribution pattern): attribution is captured via the SP Editor column (the SP-side identity of the click — PM or TPM); HILDA records the per-modality dispatch in `CommunicationLog` per FR-42 with the same Editor identity for audit. **Idempotency**: re-click re-sends the reminder (intentional — TPM ad-hoc trigger per FR-14 / FR-65); each click writes a new `last_reminder_triggered_at` timestamp and HILDA increments `reminder_count` accordingly. This action is the SP UI surface for the ad-hoc reminder capability stated in FR-14 and the manual trigger capability in FR-31.

### Submission (Stage 5)

- **FR-18** `[Ph-1]` — **UPDATED 2026-06-17 — item selection cascade with FR-63 multi-round semantic; FR-86 canonical NSD path; FR-56 (g) TPM override added to Ph-2 is_final sources; per-item dispatch atomicity + partial-success behavior; PLM upload phase split per FR-13 + FR-62 + FR-68; CommunicationLog per-dispatch payload spec; carrier scope clarification (no review attestations / no cover sheet)**. **Item selection (cascade with FR-63 lock 2026-06-17)**: HILDA selects DeliveryItems currently in `ReadyForSubmission` state at dispatch time, excluding: (a) items with `no_customer_upload = true` (per FR-80 — no carrier-submission path); (b) default work-item (`item_type = Default` per FR-78 — never reaches `ReadyForSubmission`; documents are unrouted-bucket only); (c) Confirmation items (`item_type = Confirmation` per FR-7 — no documents to dispatch). **Multi-round semantic**: items already in `SubmittedToCustomer` from a prior FR-63 Submit click are NOT re-dispatched; they're skipped (audit per `CommunicationLog action_type: resubmission` per FR-63 indicates which items were previously submitted). **Carrier scope**: HILDA dispatches only the deliverable document files per FR-86 classified path — **LLM review attestations are NOT dispatched to carrier** (they stay in HILDA's audit trail per FR-53 / FR-60 PM Review section per `[D-074]`); **no milestone-level cover sheet** is generated or dispatched (Ph-1 lock — out of scope; can be revisited Ph-2+ if carrier requires). HILDA resolves the target document revision for each selected item from the NSD `classified` path per FR-86 canonical: `<NSD_root>/internal/<carrier>/<device>/<milestone>/<tg>/<item>/<doc_type>/<doc_id_slug>/revN/` — the source of truth for in-progress deliverables (anchors `[D-041]`). Only files at the `classified` path-type per FR-86 are eligible for dispatch; files in `staged_not_classified` / `staged_not_revision` / `unrouted` paths are NOT dispatched (those paths await FR-87 TPM resolution before reaching `classified`); `[Ph-1]` one revision per document exists (rev1 only) — assembly uses the single available revision with no `is_final` check required; `[Ph-2]` only revisions with `is_final = true` in the document index are eligible for assembly — the `is_final` flag is set by EITHER (a) FR-66 owner version-selection workflow OR (b) FR-56 (g) TPM override in the HILDA-rendered PM Review section per `[D-074]` — both before the item reaches `ReadyForSubmission` (assembly relies on `is_final` being set; FR-56 (e) PM Approve transition validates that exactly one revision per `doc_id_slug` has `is_final = true` for items with multi-revision history). HILDA submits **individual files only** to the carrier portal — **zip files are never uploaded** (anchors `[D-054]`). HILDA downloads the selected individual files from NSD to the HILDA PC (where HILDA services run). **Folder structure on the carrier portal is determined by FR-77 Type-2 routing** — upload destination per FR-77: `<carrier>/<target_folder>/<matched_source_folder>/<subtree>/<filename>`. FR-69 `portal_structure.yaml` retains its role for **carrier mechanics** — `file_overwrite_policy` and `revision_file_suffix_pattern` applied at dispatch time after FR-77 determines the path: if `overwrite`, the file is uploaded with its `original_filename` (same-name upload replaces the existing file on the carrier side); if `rename`, HILDA applies `revision_file_suffix_pattern` to the `original_filename` before upload to produce a distinct carrier-side filename — e.g., `original_filename = testreport.pdf`, `revision_file_suffix_pattern = _rev{N}` → carrier filename = `testreport_rev2.pdf`; the `original_filename` in the HILDA document index is always the filename as received (never modified); the carrier-side filename is logged in `CommunicationLog` alongside the `action_type` per FR-63. HILDA dispatches per the customer's `customer_delivery_modality` using the customer adapter (per FR-19 — Ph-1/Ph-2 = Google Drive only). **Per-item dispatch atomicity + partial-success behavior**: dispatch is per-item — each item's dispatch is independent. On dispatch success for an item: `delivery_state` advances to `SubmittedToCustomer`; `CommunicationLog` records `action_type = submission` or `resubmission` per FR-63 with per-item dispatch payload (see below). On dispatch failure for an item (network error, carrier-side rejection, file-not-found at NSD): item REMAINS in `ReadyForSubmission`; failure logged to `HildaOpsAlert` per FR-75 with the carrier-side error; next FR-63 Submit click will retry only the still-`ReadyForSubmission` items. **No transactional rollback** — successfully-dispatched items are NOT reverted on subsequent-item failures (carrier-side dispatch is non-reversible by HILDA; the carrier received the file). **`CommunicationLog` per-dispatch payload**: HILDA writes one `CommunicationLog` row per dispatched file (NOT per item — an item may have multiple files) with: `action_type ∈ {submission, resubmission}` per FR-63; `delivery_item_id`; `file_hash`; `original_filename`; `carrier_side_filename` (= `original_filename` if `file_overwrite_policy = overwrite`; = `original_filename` + `revision_file_suffix_pattern` substitution if `rename` per FR-69); `carrier_target_path` per FR-77 (full upload path); `dispatch_timestamp`; `customer_adapter_run_id` (FR-19); `pm_approval_pm_id` from the row's SP-side audit (per `[D-068]`). Per-file granularity supports FR-68 verification (one hash per file) + FR-19 retry idempotency. **PLM upload trigger phase split** (cascade with FR-13 + FR-62 + FR-68): **`[Ph-1]`** docs are uploaded to PLM at ingest time per FR-13 (each ingest channel uploads immediately on classified-path write); FR-68 post-dispatch verification merely cross-checks `PLM hash = NSD hash = carrier hash`; PLM upload is NOT triggered by FR-18 dispatch. **`[Ph-2]`** PLM upload is DEFERRED per FR-62 (intermediate revisions never go to PLM; only `is_final = true` revision uploaded post-dispatch); FR-68 post-dispatch verification phase TRIGGERS the deferred PLM upload of the `is_final` revision (anchors `[D-040]`); PLM-Carrier hash verification follows. PLM upload is SKIPPED entirely (both phases) when `no_customer_upload = true` per FR-80 — those items don't go to carrier and don't go to PLM (NSD-only lifecycle). After successful dispatch, FR-68 PLM-NSD and PLM-Carrier hash sync verification fires.
- **FR-19** `[Ph-1]` — **UPDATED 2026-06-17 — run-id contract + retry idempotency; Protocol method mapping for FR-18; PM credential storage per `[D-038]`; structured `CarrierAdapterError` catalogue; worker pool concurrency control; `NotImplementedError` capability-flag handling; FR-68 hash semantics**. Customer adapters implement the surface `{submitItem, getStatus, postComment, uploadAttachment}` and authenticate as the PM using the PM's stored credentials (never a service account). **Ph-1/Ph-2 scope: Google Drive only** (carriers that accept submissions via shared Google Drive folders); web portal and JIRA-as-customer-portal adapter flavors are deferred to Ph-3+. **Google Drive REST API is not available** (corp/carrier policy constraint); customer adapter implementations use **browser-automation libraries** — `selenium` (default implementation choice) or equivalent (e.g., `playwright`, `puppeteer`); each carrier's adapter drives a headless Chromium browser instance authenticated as the PM via stored session cookies / SAML token / configured login flow per `customizations/customer_adapter/<carrier_slug>_adapter_config.yaml`. **Operational implications**: (a) per-upload latency is **10–100× slower** than a REST-API equivalent — concurrent uploads use a worker pool sized per available browser sessions (see Worker pool concurrency below); (b) selectors are versioned in adapter config to absorb Google Drive UI changes without code release; (c) `getStatus` and `postComment` may be limited or unavailable if the carrier portal surfaces these only in rendered HTML (per-carrier capability flag in adapter config — adapter raises `NotImplementedError` for unsupported operations); (d) **browser binary (Chromium) is a hard ops dependency** on the HILDA PC — must be installed before `hilda-worker` container starts (or shipped in the container image). **Protocol method mapping for FR-18 dispatch**: HILDA's FR-18 dispatch invokes **`uploadAttachment(file_path, carrier_target_path, original_filename, file_overwrite_policy, revision_file_suffix_pattern) → upload_result`** for each individual file in the package — one call per file (matches FR-18 per-file `CommunicationLog` payload granularity). `submitItem` is reserved for any item-level metadata submission (e.g., a separate carrier-side form to declare "item X is now submitted with these files attached") and is NOT invoked in Ph-1/Ph-2 Google Drive flow (Google Drive has no item-level submission gesture — file upload IS the submission); per-carrier adapters that need item-level metadata can override `submitItem`; Ph-1 Google Drive base class makes it a no-op (returns success without action). `getStatus` is invoked by FR-68 PLM-Carrier verification (read carrier-side state for hash + file-existence checks). `postComment` is invoked by `[D-044]` PM commentary flow (Ph-2+; raises `NotImplementedError` in Ph-1 Google Drive base — Google Drive has no comment surface). **Run-id contract + retry idempotency** (cascade with FR-18 `customer_adapter_run_id` payload lock 2026-06-17): each customer-adapter invocation (one Protocol-method call) is assigned a `customer_adapter_run_id` (UUID v4 generated by HILDA's dispatch coordinator before adapter call); the run_id is logged to `CommunicationLog` per FR-18 payload. **Retry idempotency**: if a previous adapter invocation crashed or timed out before HILDA could confirm carrier-side success, retries must be idempotent — adapter implementations check carrier-side state before uploading (e.g., list-files-in-target-folder + filename match) and skip re-upload if the file already exists at the FR-77 target path with matching hash. If `file_overwrite_policy = overwrite` (FR-69), retry-overwrite is safe; if `rename` with `revision_file_suffix_pattern`, retry must use the SAME suffix substitution as the original attempt (deterministic — based on `rev_number` from `DocumentIndexRow`, not a counter). Adapter MUST NOT generate per-retry unique filenames (would create N copies under retry storms). **PM credential storage** (cross-ref FR-51 + `[D-038]` + `[D-088]`): PM credentials per-carrier (Google Drive SAML cookies / OAuth refresh tokens / configured login session) are stored in **sops-encrypted env files** per `[D-038]` — `customizations/credentials/<assigned_pm_id>/<carrier_slug>.env.sops` where `<assigned_pm_id>` is the slug resolved per `[D-088]` from `Projects.TPM` (= `TPM.User_name`; fallback email-local-part of `TPM.Work_email` when null) (encrypted at rest; decrypted at adapter-instantiation time using HILDA service's sops key). PM credentials are NEVER logged, NEVER appear in `CommunicationLog` / `HildaOpsAlert` / dashboard renders per NFR-2 (no credential material in logs). Adapter implementations MUST scrub credential fields before any error message surfaces. On credential expiry (e.g., session cookie refresh failure), adapter raises `CarrierAdapterError(error_code = CSA-E001, retryable = false, message = "PM credentials expired for <carrier_slug>; ops must re-encrypt sops env file")` — surfaces to TPM dashboard per FR-75 with the PM identifier (NOT the credential). **Failure semantics — structured `CarrierAdapterError`** (cascade with FR-18 partial-success lock 2026-06-17 + FR-75 HildaOpsAlert): adapter implementations raise `CarrierAdapterError(error_code: str, retryable: bool, message: str, cause: Exception | None)` on any failure. **Error codes**: **`CSA-E000`** (credential not configured for this PM/carrier — `retryable = false`; raised by dispatch coordinator pre-flight check, BEFORE adapter invocation, when `customizations/credentials/<assigned_pm_id>/<carrier_slug>.env.sops` is missing — per FR-20 lock 2026-06-17); **`CSA-E001`** (credential expired — `retryable = false`; raised during adapter session when credentials are no longer valid); **`CSA-E002`** (carrier portal unreachable / network — `retryable = true`); **`CSA-E003`** (carrier portal selector match failure — likely UI version change; `retryable = false` until adapter config is updated); **`CSA-E004`** (carrier-side file-size / format rejection — `retryable = false`); **`CSA-E005`** (browser session crashed / stale-element — `retryable = true` after session restart); **`CSA-E006`** (file-not-found at NSD source path — `retryable = false`; NSD-side issue); **`CSA-E007`** (FR-77 target path resolution failure — `retryable = false`; `portal_structure.yaml` issue); **`CSA-W001`** (informational — `getStatus` not implemented for this carrier; FR-68 verification skipped per capability flag). HILDA's dispatch coordinator handles `retryable = true` errors with NFR-10 backoff (default 3 attempts with exponential backoff before logging to `HildaOpsAlert` per FR-75 + leaving item in `ReadyForSubmission`); `retryable = false` errors fail immediately + log to `HildaOpsAlert` + leave item in `ReadyForSubmission` for TPM intervention. **Worker pool concurrency**: default browser-session pool size is **3 concurrent sessions per carrier** (balance between dispatch throughput and HILDA PC resource consumption — each Chromium session typically uses ~200 MB RAM + 1 CPU core). Per-carrier override via `customizations/customer_adapter/<carrier_slug>_adapter_config.yaml` field `max_concurrent_sessions: <int>` (range 1–10). Global cap across all carrier adapters: **8 concurrent sessions** (configurable in HILDA's `hilda_config.yaml` `customer_adapter.global_max_concurrent_sessions`); if global cap is hit, per-carrier dispatches queue FIFO.
